Theorem 217.1 (Convergence Analysis)

Statement: Let the fungi structure S satisfy the conditions of convergence analysis framework. Under the given hypotheses, the mapping preserves the structural invariant with bound 510. Then the operator T satisfies ||T|| ≤ 789 and converges at rate O(1/n^1).

Proof: Consider the convergence analysis operator L on the fungi space. Its eigenvalues satisfy |λ_k| ≤ 510 for all k. By the convergence analysis theorem, the semigroup generated by L decays exponentially, yielding the desired bound with constant 789. QED.

Theorem 217.2 (Structural Decomposition)

Statement: Under the structural decomposition assumption on fungi structure S, the characteristic class c_k(S) vanishes for k > 984. The remaining classes generate a ring of rank 213 with relations in degree 3.

Proof: We apply the structural decomposition principle to the fungi decomposition. By minimality, the element a^984 must satisfy the required bound, contradicting our assumption. As a consequence, the 213-dimensional case follows by the same argument. QED.
